>

> - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header

> [L12] - Enforce REAL TSV: every table row MUST be tab-delimited (\t). Never space-align.
> [L13] - Summary MUST be Key\tValue lines (no "Summary", no "TSV" label lines).
> [L14] - Add SPEED_TIMEOUT gate: if parsing is not complete quickly, QA FAIL immediately with fix_hint.
> [L15] - On any QA FAIL, output must include a ready-to-upload patch instruction: "Update IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt with this FIX."
> [L16]

> [L19] - Typical native-text PDF finishes fast; otherwise fail fast with QA FAIL + fix_hint.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file8
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.5_FAST_TSV_WITH_TIMING
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce two Google-Sheets paste-ready TSV outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L9] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L13] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L14] 
> [L15] EXECUTION (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L16] 1) Start timer (ms).
> [L17] 2) Parse metadata (bureau, report_date, fico if present).
> [L18] 3) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only; ignore INQUIRY rows).
> [L19] 4) Compute fields.
> [L20] 5) Sort once by Opening Date oldest→newest (undated last stable).
> [L21] 6) Emit Output A then Output B immediately after sort.
> [L22] 7) Run only minimal hard gates (format + contract). If fail => QA FAIL only with gate_id + sample + fix_hint.
> [L23] 
> [L24] AUTHORITY FILES
> [L25] - SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L26] - QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L27] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> [L4] You've shown recent use of credit cards and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts.
> [L5] FICO® Scores evaluate the mix of credit cards, installment loans and mortgages. People who demonstrate recent and responsible use of credit cards
> [L6] and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts are generally considered less risky to lenders.
> [L7] What's hurting
> [L8]  Short revolving history
> [L9] You have not established a long revolving and/or open-ended account credit history.
> [L10] Your ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account was opened: 6 Years, 11 Months ago
> [L11] FICO High Achievers opened their ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account 25 years ago, on average.
> [L12] People with longer credit histories who infrequently open new accounts generally pose less risk to lenders. In your case, the age of your oldest revolving
> [L13] and/or open-ended account and/or the average age of your revolving and/or open-ended accounts is relatively low. <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 53 / 53> Experian 12/17/25, 3:06 PM
> [L14] https://usa.experian.com/mfe/credit/printable-report/transunion/202512172303312920 Page 53 of 53
> [L15] Prepared For ALAN NGUYEN Date generated: Dec 17, 2025
> [L16] Disclaimer
> [L17] About your FICO Score 8 or other FICO Scores
> [L18] Your FICO Score 8 powered by Experian data is formulated using the information in your credit ,le at the time it is requested. Many but not all
> [L19] lenders use FICO Score 8. In addition to the FICO Score 8, we may offer and provide other base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores (such as
> [L20] FICO Auto Scores and FICO Bankcard Scores). The other FICO Scores made available are calculated from versions of the base and
> [L21] industry-speci,c FICO Score models.
> [L22] Base FICO Scores (including the FICO Score 8) range from 300 to 850. Industry-speci,c FICO Scores range from 250-900. Higher scores
> [L23] represent a greater likelihood that you'll pay back your debts so you are viewed as being a lower credit risk to lenders. A lower FICO Score
> [L24] indicates to lenders that you may be a higher credit risk. There are many scoring models used in the marketplace. The type of score used, and
> [L25] its associated risk levels, may vary from lender to lender. But regardless of what scoring model is used, they all have one purpose: to
> [L26] summarize your creditworthiness. Keep in mind that your score is just one factor used in the application process. Other factors, such as your
> [L27] annual salary and length of employment, may also be considered by lenders when you apply for a loan.
> [L28] What this means to you:
> [L29] Credit scoring can help you understand your overall credit rating and help companies better understand how to serve you. Overall bene,ts of
> [L30] credit scoring have included faster credit approvals, reduction in human error and bias, consistency, and better terms and rates for American
> [L31] consumers through reduced costs and losses for lenders. Your lender or insurer may use a different FICO Score than FICO Score 8 or other
> [L32] base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores provided by us, or different scoring models to determine how you score.

> Content Snippet:
> [L1] IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt
> [L2] 
> [L3] RULE:
> [L4] Read this file before responding. Apply the latest FIX. Keep outputs short.
> [L5] 
> [L6] LATEST ISSUE:
> [L7] - Too slow (minutes) on native-text PDF.
> [L8] - Output not true TSV (spaces instead of TABs), not 1-click Google Sheets paste-ready.
> [L9] - No auto-reintegratable fix output on failure.
> [L10] 
> [L11] FIX:
> [L12] - Enforce REAL TSV: every table row MUST be tab-delimited (\t). Never space-align.
> [L13] - Summary MUST be Key\tValue lines (no "Summary", no "TSV" label lines).
> [L14] - Add SPEED_TIMEOUT gate: if parsing is not complete quickly, QA FAIL immediately with fix_hint.
> [L15] - On any QA FAIL, output must include a ready-to-upload patch instruction: "Update IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt with this FIX."
> [L16] 
> [L17] EXPECTED:
> [L18] - Paste into Google Sheets aligns perfectly on first paste.
> [L19] - Typical native-text PDF finishes fast; otherwise fail fast with QA FAIL + fix_hint.
> [L20] - Failures auto-produce a reintegratable fix hint.
> # 【1†MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt†file-6b4gNyguZhShfz4JoSuHan】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> Content Snippet:
> [L1] IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt
> [L2] 
> [L3] RULE:
> [L4] Read this file before responding. Apply the latest FIX. Keep outputs short.
> [L5] 
> [L6] LATEST ISSUE:
> [L7] - Too slow (minutes) on native-text PDF.
> [L8] - Output not true TSV (spaces instead of TABs), not 1-click Google Sheets paste-ready.
> [L9] - No auto-reintegratable fix output on failure.
> [L10]

> [L13] - Summary MUST be Key\tValue lines (no "Summary", no "TSV" label lines).
> [L14] - Add SPEED_TIMEOUT gate: if parsing is not complete quickly, QA FAIL immediately with fix_hint.
> [L15] - On any QA FAIL, output must include a ready-to-upload patch instruction: "Update IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt with this FIX."
> [L16] 
> [L17] EXPECTED:
> [L18] - Paste into Google Sheets aligns perfectly on first paste.
> [L19] - Typical native-text PDF finishes fast; otherwise fail fast with QA FAIL + fix_hint.

> # 【5†MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt†file-6b4gNyguZhShfz4JoSuHan】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file9
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.5
> [L2] 
> [L3] SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)
> [L4] - Each line MUST be: Key\tValue (exactly ONE tab).
> [L5] - No "Summary Key Value" header.
> [L6] - Summary is part of the contract blocks (allowed).
> [L7] 
> [L8] REQUIRED SUMMARY KEYS (IN THIS ORDER)
> [L9] Report Date
> [L10] Bureau
> [L11] FICO Score 8
> [L12] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L13] Total Open Accounts
> [L14] Total Closed Accounts
> [L15] Total Accounts
> [L16] Grand Total Debt
> [L17] Total Revolving Debt
> [L18] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L19] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L20] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L21] 
> [L22] TIMING KEYS (REQUIRED; ms; computed by model)
> [L23] Start Time (ISO)
> [L24] Parse Time (ms)
> [L25] Sort Time (ms)
> [L26] Emit Time (ms)
> [L27] Total Time (ms)
> [L28] 
> [L29] TSV HEADER (EXACT; 19 columns)
> [L30] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L31] 
> [L32] KEY RULES
> [L33] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, exactly 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L34] - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).
> [L35] - Utilization + tiers ONLY when Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L36] - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> [L37] - Allowed tier labels only:
> [L38]   Excellent (0%)
> [L39]   Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40]   Low (4–7%)
> [L41]   Review (8–9%)
> [L42]   Medium (10%+)
> [L43]   Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44]   High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE
> [L47] - Tradeline rows MUST be tab-delimited; never space-separated.
> # 【10†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-Anne3UGRKfcD8AseR9foUH】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file10
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.5_FAST_TSV_WITH_TIMING
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce two Google-Sheets paste-ready TSV outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L9] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L13] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L14] 
> [L15] EXECUTION (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L16] 1) Start timer (ms).
> [L17] 2) Parse metadata (bureau, report_date, fico if present).
> [L18] 3) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only; ignore INQUIRY rows).
> [L19] 4) Compute fields.
> [L20] 5) Sort once by Opening Date oldest→newest (undated last stable).
> [L21] 6) Emit Output A then Output B immediately after sort.
> [L22] 7) Run only minimal hard gates (format + contract). If fail => QA FAIL only with gate_id + sample + fix_hint.
> [L23] 
> [L24] AUTHORITY FILES
> [L25] - SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L26] - QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L27] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> # 【1†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-Anne3UGRKfcD8AseR9foUH】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.5_FAST_TSV_WITH_TIMING
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce two Google-Sheets paste-ready TSV outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L9] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L13] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L14] 
> [L15] EXECUTION (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L16] 1) Start timer (ms).
> [L17] 2) Parse metadata (bureau, report_date, fico if present).
> [L18] 3) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only; ignore INQUIRY rows).
> [L19] 4) Compute fields.
> [L20] 5) Sort once by Opening Date oldest→newest (undated last stable).
> [L21] 6) Emit Output A then Output B immediately after sort.
> [L22] 7) Run only minimal hard gates (format + contract). If fail => QA FAIL only with gate_id + sample + fix_hint.
> [L23] 
> [L24] AUTHORITY FILES
> [L25] - SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L26] - QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L27] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> # 【2†SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt†file-TrmDU3EQSp5dzwC3yj25pi】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report TSV extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] OUTPUT (HARD)
> [L4] - Output ONLY the contract blocks:
> [L5]   OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY  (Summary TSV + Tradelines TSV)
> [L6]   OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED (Summary TSV + Tradelines TSV)
> [L7]   OR output ONLY QA FAIL.
> [L8] - No narration, no markdown.
> [L9] 
> [L10] SPEED (HARD)
> [L11] - Parse once → sort once → emit.
> [L12] - Do NOT do multi-pass verification.
> [L13] - If unreadable/fragmented/scanned => FAIL FAST with QA FAIL.
> [L14] 
> [L15] TSV (HARD)
> [L16] - All tables MUST use literal TAB characters between columns.
> [L17] - Never space-align columns.
> # 【3†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-8z5g4NR1KfZfr5ngPX3cmu】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file3
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.5 (MINIMAL + TSV ENFORCEMENT)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT SHAPE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] - Must output OUTPUT A then OUTPUT B, or QA FAIL only.
> [L9] - Must not output narration.
> [L10] 
> [L11] TAB ENFORCEMENT (HARD FAIL)
> [L12] Let Ncols = 19 => expected_tabs = 18
> [L13] GATE_TSV_HEADER_TABS: header must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L14] GATE_TSV_ROW_TABS: each data row must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L15] GATE_SUMMARY_LINE_TABS: each summary line must contain exactly 1 TAB character.
> [L16] 
> [L17] TIER GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L18] GATE_TIER_LABEL_SET: any non-blank tier must be in allowed set.
> [L19] GATE_TIER_ON_NON_REVOLVING: tier must be blank unless Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L20] 
> [L21] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L22] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L23] Reason\tDetails
> [L24] <gate_id>\tExpected=<expected>; Observed=<observed>; Sample=<sample_line>; Fix=<fix_hint>
> # 【4†IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt†file-R4ry6B2km8tkFXk4qv4DNc】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt
> [L2] 
> [L3] RULE:
> [L4] Read this file before responding. Apply the latest FIX. Keep outputs short.
> [L5] 
> [L6] LATEST ISSUE:
> [L7] - Too slow (minutes) on native-text PDF.
> [L8] - Output not true TSV (spaces instead of TABs), not 1-click Google Sheets paste-ready.
> [L9] - No auto-reintegratable fix output on failure.
> [L10] 
> [L11] FIX:
> [L12] - Enforce REAL TSV: every table row MUST be tab-delimited (\t). Never space-align.
> [L13] - Summary MUST be Key\tValue lines (no "Summary", no "TSV" label lines).
> [L14] - Add SPEED_TIMEOUT gate: if parsing is not complete quickly, QA FAIL immediately with fix_hint.
> [L15] - On any QA FAIL, output must include a ready-to-upload patch instruction: "Update IMPROVEMENTS_AND_FIXES.txt with this FIX."
> [L16] 
> [L17] EXPECTED:
> [L18] - Paste into Google Sheets aligns perfectly on first paste.
> [L19] - Typical native-text PDF finishes fast; otherwise fail fast with QA FAIL + fix_hint.
> [L20] - Failures auto-produce a reintegratable fix hint.
> # 【5†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-5nVtnvQRm2rq4C3aaX1eYm】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.5
> [L2] 
> [L3] SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)
> [L4] - Each line MUST be: Key\tValue (exactly ONE tab).
> [L5] - No "Summary Key Value" header.
> [L6] - Summary is part of the contract blocks (allowed).
> [L7] 
> [L8] REQUIRED SUMMARY KEYS (IN THIS ORDER)
> [L9] Report Date
> [L10] Bureau
> [L11] FICO Score 8
> [L12] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L13] Total Open Accounts
> [L14] Total Closed Accounts
> [L15] Total Accounts
> [L16] Grand Total Debt
> [L17] Total Revolving Debt
> [L18] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L19] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L20] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L21] 
> [L22] TIMING KEYS (REQUIRED; ms; computed by model)
> [L23] Start Time (ISO)
> [L24] Parse Time (ms)
> [L25] Sort Time (ms)
> [L26] Emit Time (ms)
> [L27] Total Time (ms)
> [L28] 
> [L29] TSV HEADER (EXACT; 19 columns)
> [L30] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L31] 
> [L32] KEY RULES
> [L33] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, exactly 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L34] - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).
> [L35] - Utilization + tiers ONLY when Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L36] - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> [L37] - Allowed tier labels only:
> [L38]   Excellent (0%)
> [L39]   Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40]   Low (4–7%)
> [L41]   Review (8–9%)
> [L42]   Medium (10%+)
> [L43]   Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44]   High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE
> [L47] - Tradeline rows MUST be tab-delimited; never space-separated.
> # 【6†MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt†file-6b4gNyguZhShfz4JoSuHan】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
